## Current Status Summary

`pocr_tri_engine_pilot_annotation_replay_aggregation_v0` is complete under `audits/pocr_tri_engine_pilot_annotation_replay_aggregation_v0/` as the first checkpointed tri-engine POCR promotion pilot. D039 was reused; `DECISION_LOG.md` was not modified. The pilot used the previously approved 5 cases x 3 engines x 2 routes plan: `PERF_0006`, `CONS_0005`, `PORT_0003`, `LONGTAIL_0011`, and `LONGTAIL_0022`; engines PostgreSQL, MySQL, and Spark; routes Direct LLM Repair-1 and SQLGlot no-op sanity/control. Candidate-bound rows were 30/30. The checkpointed runner made 30 live API calls under the explicit live gate, wrote 30 local annotation JSONL rows under uncommitted D035 `output/`, and produced 21 schema-valid rows plus 9 fail-closed rows (8 malformed JSON, 1 provider call failure). User-facing `sqlrb user pocr-diagnostic` replay ran for all six route-engine combinations, emitted 30 diagnostic rows, wrote six durable `pocr_stage_b_row_metrics.csv` files, and preserved route mismatch rows 0 and candidate mismatch rows 0. The promotion-diagnostic aggregator wrote a local `pocr_route_summary.csv` with Direct LLM Repair-1 diagnostic macro values by engine: PostgreSQL `POCR@planned=0.066666666667` / `POCR@candidate=0.066666666667`, MySQL `0.200000000000` / `0.200000000000`, Spark `0.000000000000` / `0.000000000000`; SQLGlot no-op was `0.000000000000` for both views on all three engines with 0 possible over-accept cases. These are promotion-diagnostic values only: this is not official POCR, no route-level official POCR score was emitted, no paper-facing metric was promoted, and `POCR@curated` remains deferred until a predeclared curated manifest exists. No DB/checker/timing run, baseline rerun, candidate SQL generation or mutation, top-level reports/results update, retained-evidence promotion, output commit, denominator change, case membership change, paper result change, raw legacy evidence change, or leaderboard output occurred. Next safe action: if pilot quality gates are accepted, decide whether to retry fail-closed pilot annotations, perform a Stage B quality review, or move to PG40 official-pilot design.

`pocr_official_metric_promotion_design_v0` is complete under `audits/pocr_official_metric_promotion_design_v0/` as a POCR official metric promotion design audit. Root cause category: Spark local diagnostic statement splitter / preflight consistency gap. SQLGlot noop emitted one query with leading block comments and a trailing semicolon; those block comments preserved metadata/provenance semicolons. Candidate preflight is block-comment-aware and passed all six rows, while the Spark diagnostic splitter strips only full-line `--` comments and split on semicolons inside `/* ... */` comments, producing two to four fragments. Source execution succeeded because source comments remained full-line `--` comments.
